# Backlog

Work we've decided on but haven't built. One line per item. No statuses, no priorities, no IDs — if it's here it's open, when it ships delete the line.

This is not the record of the project. The **why** lives in [decisions/](decisions/) (find the ADR for your domain via the router in [INDEX.md](INDEX.md)); the **what happened** lives in [planning/](planning/) and the git history. See [ADR-0046](decisions/0046-retire-the-task-board.md) for why it's this small.

## Sharing & exports

- **The `ltrIsolate`-around-a-Hebrew-phrase sweep is DONE for the shapes lint can see, and open for the rest** (2026-08-31, [ADR-0213](decisions/0213-a-shared-trip-changes-emphasis-and-print-is-its-own-rendering.md) eighth amendment §4). Ten sites fixed and two ESLint selectors added beside the existing `dir="ltr"` one — a Hebrew letter inside an isolated template, and a call to `hoursPhrase`/`pdfSpan`/`approxDuration`/`approxTravelTime` inside one. What the guard still cannot see is an isolate around a **variable** that happens to hold Hebrew at runtime: `ltrIsolate(due.day)` was caught only because the template literal was written out at the call site, and `dayLabel`'s Hebrew return is invisible to a selector. The remaining shape is a helper whose Hebrew-ness is one indirection away, and finding those wants a runtime probe (temporarily throw from `ltrIsolate` on a Hebrew argument, run the suites) rather than more selectors — that measurement was set up in this session and not spent.

- **The `dir="auto"`-on-a-value-block sweep, beyond sharing** (fixed in both sharing renderers 2026-08-30, [ADR-0213](decisions/0213-a-shared-trip-changes-emphasis-and-print-is-its-own-rendering.md) fourth amendment §8; the rest is open). `dir="auto"` sets the element's BASE DIRECTION, and base direction drives `text-align: start` as well as bidi resolution — so `<strong dir="auto">Grjótagjá cave</strong>` lands 229px from the start edge of a 288px column while its own Hebrew caption sits at 0. The fix is `autoIsolate` (FSI…PDI) from `lib/bidi.ts`, whose docblock covers a **composed line joining several values** and therefore left the case of a single value alone in a block uncovered — which is why this survived ADR-0118's sweep. **The shape is anywhere a foreign-script value is the whole content of its own element**, so it wants a repo-wide grep rather than another per-surface fix.

- **The route could be anchored to regions now that regions exist.** `routeLabels` still derives from each day's first stop, so it names waterfalls and craters where a reader expects areas. Both renderers stopped printing it (2026-08-30), but `buildSummaryNarrativeInput` still consumes it — and the region concept it lacked now exists as an enrichment field ([ADR-0166](decisions/0166-place-enrichment-is-a-multi-source-pipe.md) §23, Wikidata `P131`), already feeding the day titles.

- **One `.invite-box`, one plan-violet cleanup left** — `ui/TripLinkRow.tsx` now serves the share sheet and Trip Settings (ADR-0213's 2026-08-30 amendment), but the **born screen** (`screens/CreateTrip.tsx`) still renders `.invite-box`, which paints `--plan-tint` and a dashed violet border outside Plan mode. It is the one remaining copy and the one remaining colour-budget spend on that element; the born screen's arrival animation hangs off the box (`birth-arr`, `--i`), so swapping it is a motion question as well as a styling one.

- **User-typed names on a share link, and two links at one policy** — the original access-management item, narrowed twice by the tenth amendment. A _name_ is now an addition to the derived policy title (`קודים · פתקים · 2 קבצים`) rather than a replacement for it: the title says what the link reveals, which the app can verify; a typed name says who it went to, which it cannot and which goes stale silently. _Two links at one policy_ — independently revocable per recipient — is the part that genuinely needs a list screen and a divergence policy, and is not what was asked for.

- **A PDF at a policy with no link mints that link** ([ADR-0213](decisions/0213-a-shared-trip-changes-emphasis-and-print-is-its-own-rendering.md) tenth amendment §7). Already true before the amendment (`ensureShare` runs before the render) and sharper now that every toggle combination is a possible address: wanting a file publishes a live URL. Rendering a projection from a policy that has no row is the change.
- **A configured narrative provider, and the policy question under it** — the `ItineraryNarrativeGenerator` port ships bound to `DisabledItineraryNarrativeGenerator`, so every shared page and PDF runs on the deterministic fallback ([ADR-0213](decisions/0213-a-shared-trip-changes-emphasis-and-print-is-its-own-rendering.md) §2). Swapping that one line in `sharing.module.ts` is the whole integration surface. What is genuinely undecided is the provider: an allowlist cannot prove arbitrary prose is non-sensitive, so external generation is limited to Summary-public text — and a promise that **no** itinerary content leaves Travelive would require a local or self-hosted model rather than a broader filter.

- **A device pass on the shared reader's landing peek** (2026-08-31, [ADR-0213](decisions/0213-a-shared-trip-changes-emphasis-and-print-is-its-own-rendering.md) eleventh amendment §1, **built**). The gap the page leaves above today's card is `scroll-margin-block-start: 26px` on `.sh-day` — drawn and measured, never held in a hand. [`mockups/a-shared-itinerary-knows-what-day-it-is-v1.html`](../mockups/a-shared-itinerary-knows-what-day-it-is-v1.html) still offers 0/26/76 as a control.

- **The now-line still cannot say it is INSIDE something** — the app's own open question, now with a second host. `lib/now-line.ts` was shaped for a placement that grows an `inside` field, and until it does, a marker beside a running row is an approximation on both surfaces: `DayView` puts the line above such a row, the shared reader below it (eleventh amendment's _What building it changed_ §2, and the reasoning for the difference is there). One rule when `nowLinePlacement` grows the shape it was built to grow.

## Maps & Places epic (scope + phasing in ADR-0106)

- **A flight is a line — the two pieces left** ([ADR-0212](decisions/0212-a-flight-is-a-line-that-is-also-a-commitment.md), **built 2026-08-29**; the thread, the carried distance, the split day total, the aligned icon column and the thousands separator all shipped). **(1)** The thread is **Trip mode's only** — Plan gets both facts but not the line, because its rows sit inside the reorder/drag machinery no drawing has covered; that is a mockup and a decision of its own. **(2)** Two flights that should join as an `עצירת ביניים` draw a gap chip instead — owner-confirmed wrong, cause still the untested hypothesis that the two Vienna endpoints are different place records (`a.toPlaceId !== b.fromPlaceId`). A data or place-picker question rather than a design one; nothing built depends on the answer, since the thread wraps whatever `joinBetween` already groups.

- **Detail is live, and an extract is only for the plane** ([ADR-0187](decisions/0187-detail-is-live-and-an-extract-is-only-for-the-plane.md), designed 2026-08-14 — **3a + 3b + 3c built; 3d open**). Research-only `Place` cache rows do not invalidate an extract: `MapService.coordinatesFor` cuts from places referenced by saved entities. Online z7–14 detail is a build-addressed range read of the upstream planet through our guarded backend route (the build is resolved at runtime, not pinned in the bundle — see the line below), with bounded server memory LRU and optional local-FS cache; offline uses device-held world and journey-shaped extract archives under the retention, pinning and metering rules. Search-result pins now render before selection, and offline no longer disables the map canvas. **3d remains:** a bounded client-memory LRU for arbitrary areas explored in the current session, without persisting every pan.

- **The live map build is resolved, not pinned** — **done (2026-08-21)**, from a field report: _"when online you can't see locations · bare map"_. Upstream keeps ~7 daily builds (measured: `20260815`–`20260821` served, `20260814` and older `404`), so `MAP_PLANET_BUILD = '20260813'` expired a week after it was typed and every live range read hit nothing. The server now probes the last 8 dailies, serves the newest that answers, re-resolves on a 6h TTL, and states the id on `/me` (`map.liveBuild`); the client builds the path from that and falls back to the coarse world when there is none; the route serves any build inside the retention window (ADR-0187's 2026-08-21 amendment). **Still open:** a detail source that yields no tiles while the world layer paints is invisible to `MapPane` — `isGroundSource` counts either archive, so `tilesPainted` latches and every cue renders under `!tilesPainted`. Counting tiles per source would make that class of failure loud, and it changes what "painted" means, so it is a decision rather than a follow-up.

- **An offline archive is not permanent** — **done (2026-08-21)**, the owner's follow-on to the line above: _"offline maps have to be updated too, not because they won't work but because we prefer updated maps."_ Offline artefacts now carry a **30-day vintage** derived from the live build (`MAP_ARCHIVE_VINTAGE_DAYS`, `mapArchiveVintage`): the server puts it in the storage key — so the world layer stops being cut once per deploy and frozen — states it on `/me` as `map.archiveVintage`, and a device replaces its copy when it is a vintage behind **and** older than the window. Deliberately a slower clock than the daily build (a vintage per build is 42.7 MB per device per day), and a refresh never prompts or spends metered bytes: a missing archive earns §5's prompt, a merely stale one does not (ADR-0186 §6 amendment, rule 6). **Not built:** nothing tells a person how old their map is — §5's manage row lists size, not age, and that row is where an _asked-for_ refresh would go.

- **Nothing ever purges a map archive server-side** — noticed 2026-08-21 while adding the vintage, not fixed. `isExtractKeyFor` was written "for eviction" (ADR-0186 §6) and has **no caller outside its own spec**, so every superseded archive stays in the bucket: already one per region signature, and now one per 30-day vintage per trip. The current and previous vintages are wanted (a device still holding one reads it); everything older is dead weight. A sweep needs a **key listing the byte sink does not have** — `common/storage.ts` is `getObject`/`putObject`/`deleteObject` only — so this is "add `listObjects(prefix)` to the sink, then a retention pass", not a one-liner.

- **`Retry-After` is unreadable cross-origin** — noticed 2026-08-21, not fixed. `map-archive-cache.ts`'s `retryAfterSeconds` reads the header off a 503, but it is not on the CORS response safelist and `main.ts`'s `enableCors` sets no `exposedHeaders` — so in a deployed (cross-origin API) build every "still being cut" retry waits the 5s default instead of the server's `MAP_BUILD_RETRY_SECONDS`. Harmless today (the retry still lands) and a two-line fix, but it is a header the client believes it is reading and is not.

The Map is the last unbuilt core tab. [ADR-0106](decisions/0106-maps-and-places-epic-scope-and-phasing.md) is the frame: one mode-re-emphasized tab, picker-first, list-of-pins before an embedded map, filters that are pure client-side derivation. All three follow-on sessions are done: the **design session** ([ADR-0109](decisions/0109-map-tab-design.md) + `mockups/map-tab-v1.html`: list-first tab, Index-grammar filters, category-coloured pins, geolocation UX, near-me, forms, ADR-0107's zone chip; resolves ADR-0106's open design questions), **BE-arch** ([ADR-0108](decisions/0108-maps-and-places-backend-architecture-key-model-and-cost.md): a two-key split — public browser key for the Maps JS API only; a server key for Places New + Routes behind a thin backend proxy on the existing `places` module — `geo-tz` server-side for `Place.timezone`, the `Place` row as the cache with `@@unique([tripId, googlePlaceId])`), and **FE-arch** ([ADR-0110](decisions/0110-maps-and-places-frontend-architecture.md): one shared search core [`lib/usePlaceSearch.ts` hook + two proxy calls on `lib/api.ts`], the place-usage derivation [`lib/place-usage.ts`, feeding both filter chips and pin colour], per-event zone threading through `lib/places.ts`/`lib/time.ts` [resolving ADR-0107 §7 — `Event.displayTimezone` is a manual override, derived by default], and the context-aware `setActiveDate`). **All six phases have shipped** — Phase 6, the rendered map, in session 133 to [ADR-0121](decisions/0121-embedded-map-phase-6-design.md). What is left of the epic is the **paid-Routes enhancement** sequenced after it (live ETAs; a second cost envelope, a second proxy route, and a 10-waypoint Essentials ceiling — ADR-0121 §1/§14), plus the non-code items on the Phase-6 line below (the human visual pass, the cloud styles, staging vars, and two questions left open by decision). Phase order below.

- **Google Cloud project setup** (human, Phase 0) — near-term slice **done (2026-07-23):** Places API (New) enabled on the existing project, billing budget alert + per-day request quota cap set (ADR-0108 §6 hard gate), and the server key `GOOGLE_MAPS_SERVER_KEY` (Places New, IP/server-held) minted and stored in local `.env` + Railway. **Phase 1 is unblocked.** _Deferred to Phase 6:_ enable Maps JS API + Routes API, add Routes to the server key, mint the referrer-locked browser key (`VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_BROWSER_KEY`, Maps JS only), and add the Dynamic Maps/Routes daily quota caps. **Phase-6 slice done (2026-07-26, session 132):** Maps JS API enabled, Dynamic Maps daily quota capped + budget alert confirmed against the new SKU, both Map IDs created (`waypoint-day`/`waypoint-night`, JavaScript/vector), the referrer-locked browser key minted (Maps JS only), and the three `VITE_` build vars set locally + Railway production. `engineering/prerequisites-checklist.md` carries the whole click-path. **The embedded-map build is unblocked and viewable.** Two non-blocking leftovers: the **night** style is authored (`design/map-styles/`) but not imported, which is inert until dark mode ships (the **day** style is imported and associated, and was redesigned in [ADR-0125](decisions/0125-map-canvas-terrain-vocabulary.md)); and the vars are not on Railway **staging**, so the tab is list-only there. Routes stays off until the paid work.
- **Places picker — backend slice (Phase 1)** — **done (2026-07-23):** proxy routes on the existing `places` module (`POST …/places/search` Autocomplete relay + `POST …/places/resolve` enrich-on-pick create-or-link, `enrichPlaceId?` to enrich a Place-lite in place), a new `google-places.client.ts` holding `GOOGLE_MAPS_SERVER_KEY` (via `env.ts`), dedup-before-spend on `(tripId, googlePlaceId)` (zero Google spend on a cache hit), `Place.timezone` resolved server-side via `geo-tz`, a per-member·trip rate limit (`PlacesThrottlerGuard`, `${userId}:${tripId}` tracker, per-minute + per-day windows, env-tunable) reusing `@nestjs/throttler`. Schema shipped: `Place.timezone`/`Place.rating`/`Place.userRatingsTotal` + `@@unique([tripId, googlePlaceId])`, mirrored in `@waypoint/shared` (`placeSchema` + new `placePredictionSchema`/`searchPlacesSchema`/`resolvePlaceSchema`). Field mask stays **Pro tier** and `rating`/`userRatingsTotal` are **not** fetched yet (columns exist, unpopulated) — **ADR-0111** (Enterprise-tier fields deferred until a surface renders the ★). `api-contract.md` updated.
- **Places picker — frontend core + EventForm (Phase 1)** — **done (2026-07-23):** the shared search core (ADR-0110 §1) — `lib/usePlaceSearch.ts` (FE-minted session token lazy/threaded/retired, pause-gated debounce [`PLACE_SEARCH_DEBOUNCE_MS`/`PLACE_SEARCH_MIN_CHARS`], snapshot-derived `alreadyInTrip` dedup that links without spend, soft 429, offline name-only fallback via the existing `CREATE_PLACE` outbox verb) behind a net-new `ui/primitives/PlacePicker` (search sheet via `Modal`/`useOverlay`); `searchPlaces` + `resolvePlace` (`enrichPlaceId?`) on `lib/api.ts` + `isRateLimitedError`; a `resolvePlace` `indexVerbs` verb adopts the canonical row (online-only, not outboxed; the WS `place` echo reconciles via the existing registry, ADR-0094). Wired into **EventForm** (re-adds the place-authoring hole ADR-0051 left; a picked place also threads through the shelf-schedule path). Tests for the hook + picker.
- **Places picker — frontend, BookingSheet single-place location (Phase 1)** — **done (2026-07-23):** `PlacePicker` wired into non-transport bookings (hotel/restaurant/activity/other), which authored no place before; sends `placeId` on save (mutually exclusive with transport's from/to, ADR-0048).
- **Places picker — transport origin/destination as places** (flights/trains) — **done (2026-07-24, session 86):** the flight/train endpoints are now **picked places** (coords + timezone), authored through the same `PlacePicker` as every other booking, not free-text name-only lites. In `BookingSheet` the route row reshaped (ADR-0059 §3 amendment): the two endpoints moved to a dedicated **route field** (two stacked `PlacePicker`s), the title row shows a read-only `RouteLabel` preview, and save stores `fromPlaceId`/`toPlaceId` directly (title still `origin → destination` via `routeTitle`, so every route surface is unchanged). The PlanHome free-text seed now prefills only a matching existing place (else the user picks; no orphan on open). This is the **last data prerequisite for the multi-zone model (ADR-0107)** — a TLV→NRT flight now has real `fromPlace`/`toPlace` zones to render each end in, and ADR-0107 can derive the origin/home zone from the outbound flight's `fromPlace`. Phase-6 route pins/ETAs also benefit.
- **Places picker — cached vs in-trip fix** — **done (2026-07-23):** a pick still persists a `Place` row immediately (the dedup cache), but "in the trip" is now derived from references (`referencedPlaceIds` in `lib/places.ts`), so a picked-but-cancelled place is cache-only — it no longer shows "already in trip", and re-picking still dedups at zero Google spend (ADR-0112).
- **Cross-trip global place cache** (proposed, deferred — a scale optimization) — a global `googlePlaceId → details` cache tier, separate from trip-scoped `Place` rows, so a popular place resolved in one trip is reused across trips without re-querying Google. Reverses ADR-0106's "cross-trip dedup OUT"; needs a Google-terms-compliant content TTL/refresh (place*id cacheable indefinitely, other Place fields time-limited; our geo-tz timezone is exempt). Build when volume warrants (ADR-0065 grow-later), writing the ADR then. *(Captured session 74.)\_
- **Explicit event category selector** (ADR-0109 §11 / ADR-0038 2026-07-23 amendment + follow-up) — **done (2026-07-23):** `EventForm` gets an explicit `category` selector — the same `ChoiceGrid` (`layout="pills"`) the booking-type picker uses, over `EVENT_CATEGORY_OPTIONS` (one shared options list, `lib/category-options.ts`; no colour swatch), sized up a touch (`.category-pills`) and placed **above** the icon+name row (it defaults the glyph, so it reads first). `IconPicker` is now **glyph-only** (its `onChange` dropped the category arg; the icon-derived readout + `categoryForIcon`-as-source retired); picking a category defaults the badge glyph via `iconForCategory` unless the user chose one (`iconTouched`). A booking-linked event hides the selector (category owned by the booking type). `ChoiceGrid.value` is now optional (single-select can start unset). **The maybe-shelf quick-add deliberately does _not_ get the selector** (a pills row in the one-line day-view jot is awkward, and category isn't a must for an idea) — an idea is created uncategorized and gets its category when scheduled into an event (ADR-0038 follow-up); an optional shelf-tag affordance was deferred. No schema change.
- **Places on existing surfaces** (Phase 2) — **done (2026-07-24):** Universal Maps-URL deep-link builders in `lib/places.ts` (`mapsDirectionsUrl`/`mapsPlaceUrl` + `eventDirectionsUrl`/`bookingDirectionsUrl`, all **null when the place is coordless** — no key, no Google spend, ADR-0106 §5); the `ניווט` affordance turned from a no-op stub (`verbs.navigate` toast, deleted) into a real device deep-link everywhere it appeared — `EventCard` (`onNavigate` now optional; 3 buttons gated on location, kept on read-only per ADR-0029) and `TransitionRow` (gated on `!readOnly && navigable`), both fed by `DayView`'s `navigateHandler` — **and hidden when there's no location**; `BookingDetail` gains a teal "open in Maps" link (authority rule → transport origin / single place). Navigate-to-next Home tile stays **Phase 4**; Index/board rows reach the link via `BookingDetail`. (Session 78.) **Follow-up done (2026-07-24, session 79):** split into two labelled location actions everywhere — `ניווט` (directions) + `מפה` (view); `BookingDetail` now shows the place as a `מיקום` **detail row** (name/address) carrying both links; `EventCard` gets the `ניווט · מפה` pair (`onShowOnMap` added, `mapActs` fragment); view/URL helpers `eventPlaceUrl`/`bookingPlaceUrl` added. ADR-0109 amended (2026-07-24) to scope §1's single-`נווט`+tap-to-view rule to the Map-tab list row, and to record the **interim** below.
- ~~**`מפה` (view) → in-app map, not Google**~~ — **done (2026-07-26, session 133)** with Phase 6 (ADR-0121 §8). The row tap **selects** rather than views: row and pin are one selection, and focusing is what selection does when the place has coordinates — so a **coordless** row still selects (it is still referenced, it just has no camera to move), and a row tap at full sheet height drops the sheet to half. `מפה` on an `EventCard`/`BookingDetail` routes to the Map tab focused on the place, through a pending focus on the lifted `MapScopeProvider` (the Map widens to all-days by itself when the place is not in the day it lands on). Going to Google is the `נווט` **button** (directions), which stays a deep-link permanently (ADR-0106 §F). "View on Google Maps" is **retired, not relocated**: `mapsPlaceUrl` / `eventPlaceUrl` / `bookingPlaceUrl` were removed with their last call sites rather than kept as dead exports — the surviving research case goes through `mapsPredictionUrl`, and the pair `EventCard`/`BookingDetail` need is now `eventMapPlace` / `bookingMapPlace`, which resolve a **Place** because the destination is a tab and a selection, not a URL.
- **Map tab v1 — list + filters** (Phase 3) — **done (2026-07-24, session 80):** `lib/place-usage.ts` (`buildPlaceUsageIndex` → per-place days [edge/ambient], category union, isMaybe/isScheduled, coordless, colour-by-most-committed pin; `matchesPlaceFilter`/`countPlacesByCategory`) built on the existing resolver; `CATEGORY_PIN_HUE` (9→5) + the 5 `--cat-*` tokens; `screens/Map.tsx` — the list-first surface reusing the Index grammar (`ChoiceGrid` pills type filter + independent `אולי` toggle + `SearchOverlay` + `--idx-accent`), the map-local `כל הימים` scope chip (Trip→today / Plan→all), rows per ADR-0109 §1 (category badge, name + 🔒, meta · `על המדף` · `★`, one labelled `ניווט` directions link, row-tap = view); context-aware `daySelectTarget` (`DAY_SCOPED_TABS`) so a strip day focuses the map in place (ADR-0110 §4). Category-coloured **rendered** pins (the teardrop map) remain **Phase 6**; commitment stays the hard/soft grammar, not colour.
- **Map tab list order = trip order** — **done (2026-07-25, session 106):** ADR-0109 §1 never said what ordered the rows, so the Phase-3 build used `date` then **place name** — meaning in Trip mode's one-day default scope today's map was **alphabetical**, while §6's shipped denied banner already claimed `הרשימה ממוינת לפי לו״ז`. `DayUsage` gains `at?` (the day's earliest referencing moment, an **absolute** instant — ADR-0107 makes the zone presentation, not the ordering) + `sortOrder?`, sourced from the span's edges (a stay's check-in / check-out); `comparePlacesBySchedule` (`lib/place-usage.ts`) reuses **the day view's own vocabulary** — start instant → `sortOrder`, untimed after the clocked ones as `DayView` renders them — so the map and the timeline can't disagree about a day (the ADR-0107 session-102 lesson applied to ordering). Three tiers sink below the schedule: untimed → ambient backdrop (ADR-0054) → no day at all, the last of which also fixes dateless places (unlinked bookings, shelf ideas) previously sorting to the **top** of the all-days list. A transport event's endpoints now carry their own moments (origin at departure, destination at arrival), so a flight lists in travel order. Near-me is unchanged but falls back to schedule order, not the alphabet. **No sort control and no second sort** — with the default honest, near-me stays the tab's only ordering toggle (ADR-0109 session-106 amendment).
- **Map tab: what's behind you sinks** — **done (2026-07-25, session 107):** trip order alone put a day's already-visited places above the stop you're heading to (reported from a real screenshot: at 14:11 the row ringed `היעד הבא · 17:00` was **last**). In Trip mode a place whose moment has passed now sinks to the bottom of its day — within-day order is **ahead (clocked) → untimed → ambient backdrop → behind you** — under a `כבר היינו` header reusing near-me's `.map-grouphead` (the rows carry no time yet, so a silent reorder would be unexplainable). `DayUsage` gains `until` (the **latest** end among a day's references, on `eventPhase`'s `endsAt ?? startsAt` boundary), so an in-progress event keeps its chronological lead and a stay is behind you only once check-out passes; an untimed event outranks a visited one; the sunk block stays chronological; a wholly-past day is unchanged. `comparePlacesBySchedule` now takes a `{ nameOf, onDate?, nowMs? }` context, and `isDayUsagePast`/`placeDay` are exported so the screen partitions with the same rule the comparator uses. Trip mode only — Plan mode drafts the sequence (ADR-0109 session-107 amendment).
- **Map tab: ahead of you, then behind you newest-first** — **done (2026-07-25, session 110):** session 107's sink was both buggy and wrongly ordered. The comparator ordered by **date before** the ahead/behind rank, so it only ever operated _within_ a day — in all-days scope the list still opened on the trip's earliest day (last Tuesday above tonight's stop), which survived three sessions because every test for it was day-scoped. And 107 kept the sunk block chronological, where newest-first is right. The split is now the **outermost** key: **ahead of you** (earliest first, whatever day; within a day still the day view's start-then-`sortOrder` vocabulary, ambient trailing as backdrop) then **behind you** (newest first, no within-day hierarchy — everything there is equally done), with no-day-at-all last. Two further reversals of 107: it applies in **both modes** (a list opening on last Tuesday is wrong while planning too), and a whole passed day counts as behind you (`isDayUsagePast` gained `today`) so an untimed row on a finished day can't float into the ahead block (ADR-0109 session-110 amendment).
- **Commitment-ranked place ordering** (considered, rejected for now — from session 106) — the other reading of "sort by precedence": rank the Map list hard → soft → idea. Rejected because the hard/soft grammar already marks commitment on every row (🔒, dashed), so ranking by it would restate what the row says; `place-usage.ts` already computes the weight (`pin.commitment`) if a surface ever needs it. Revisit only with a concrete case a filter can't serve; write the ADR then.
- **Map tab — deferred follow-ups** — **polish (a)+(b) done (2026-07-24, session 81):** (a) **`DayStrip` all-scope suppression** — the map-local `allDays` is now a lifted `MapScopeProvider` context; the header strip drops its filled selection (keeping the today-anchor) while all-days is on (`DayStrip allScope`); (b) **coordless `＋ מיקום` enrich-from-map** — a coordless row now opens the shared `PlacePickerSheet` (exported) to enrich it in place. **(c) done (2026-07-25, session 108):** the `<time> · <what>` meta line §1 always specified, unblocked once the ADR-0107 display track finished. `DayUsage` gains `eventId` + `edge` — the derivation only **points** at the reference owning the day's moment (following whichever won `at` on a merge), so `place-usage.ts` stays clock- and zone-free and the screen resolves the wording + zone. A bracketed booking's end says **which end** in the existing per-mode transition vocabulary (`eventEdgeTransition`, added to `lib/transitions.ts`; take-off/landing, departure/arrival, check-in/out) — not the bare transition word §1 forbids, since the row already names the place and shows the time; anything else says its title via `shortTitleText`. The **address is demoted to a fallback** for rows with nothing scheduled, and a strictly-middle stay night says nothing back (its own name is not news). The time renders in that event's own zone **per end**, so a flight reads `09:15 · המראה` / `13:00 · נחיתה`. Knock-ons: the navigate-to-next tag **dropped its now-duplicated time** (says only which row is next), and session 107's `כבר היינו` header stops being the only evidence for the reordering. **All-days day label done (2026-07-25, session 109):** in all-days scope a bare `09:00` read as today, so the day now leads the **same** amber tag (`מחר · 09:00`) via `relativeDay` (ADR-0085) in the Index row's composition; day-scoped is unchanged (the strip names the day), and an untimed event finally gains a way to say when. `relativeDayLabel` generalized out of `index-bookings.ts` into `lib/time.ts` (rule 8). Day **group headers** rejected: a place appears once under its earliest day, so a per-day header would promise completeness it can't deliver. **(d) done (2026-07-26, session 133):** **`מפה`/view → in-app map focus** — the Phase-2 `TODO(phase-3)` closed with Phase 6 as ADR-0121 §8 specified, the tap becoming the row↔pin selection rather than a separate control. Nothing on this line is deferred any more.
- **Trip-mode live jobs — navigate-to-next (Phase 4b)** — **done (2026-07-25, session 104):** the tile ADR-0045 deferred is back, now that places carry real coordinates. One derivation, `nextDestination` (`lib/places.ts`) — the earliest **timed upcoming** event whose resolved place is **mappable**, returning `{ event, place, url }` (it resolves the directions link itself, so no call site re-checks for coords); the authority rule means transport resolves to its **origin** (you go to the airport you fly from), and it looks **past** a placeless event or a coordless Place-lite, so like `nextCodedBooking` it may land on a later event than the board's immediate next. No lookahead window (same scan as the board's NEXT), and a checked-in hotel falls out of the `upcoming` filter for free. **Home:** the fourth quick-access tile (`🧭 ניווט ליעד הבא` + the stop's `shortPlaceLabel` name), an **`<a>`** not a button so the hand-off out to Maps is a real link; `QUICK_TILE_MAX_COLS` 3→4 and the grid still reflows when the tile is absent. **Map tab:** not a second control but the one time-anchor the map's colour budget allows (ADR-0109 §6's single amber ring, in list form) — an amber `היעד הבא · 18:40` tag + soft ring on exactly one row, the row's existing `נווט` being the action; the time renders in the **event's own** zone (ADR-0107), Trip mode only. Survives Phase 6 unchanged (the ring moves to the pin). _(No Home render test: the logic is unit-tested at the derivation, and Home has no render harness yet — see the session note.)_
- **Trip-mode live jobs — geolocation + near-me (Phase 4a)** — **done (2026-07-25, session 105; Phase 4 is closed):** `lib/distance.ts` (haversine + `formatDistance`, precision dropping as the number grows — 10 m steps under a kilometre, one decimal to 10 km, whole km past it, never rounding a place you're standing on to zero) and `lib/useGeolocation.ts` (`idle → locating → granted | denied | unavailable` + a **`blocked`** flag off the Permissions API; **one shot per request, not `watchPosition`** — a fix answers a question just asked, and nothing needs metre-by-metre updates; the position never leaves React state, per ADR-0006). On the tab: the chip asks for nothing on open, an **inline** reason-first card (not an overlay — it explains rather than interrupts) gates the device call; granted → nearest-first under a `לפי קרבה אליך` header with teal distance chips, coordless rows sinking last, ties falling back to the default day/name order; refused → the list keeps its own order under a dismissable banner; offline → the chip is **removed** (you can't re-locate), showing distances become `מרחק לא זמין`, rows desaturate under the existing "last saved" banner. **ADR-0109 §6 amended (session 105):** its "deep-link to the OS location settings" is impossible on the web, so the affordance is a real **retry** while the permission is still promptable and an **instruction** once hard-denied — never a button that does nothing. The sort gains the spatial "me" dot for free in Phase 6.
- **Plan-mode research** (Phase 5) — **done (2026-07-25, session 111; designed + built in [ADR-0115](decisions/0115-plan-mode-place-research.md) + `mockups/map-research-v1.html`):** search Google from the Map tab → the result becomes a pinned place → `＋ אולי` onto the shelf. The tab's **one** search control now has two halves: it opens free (filtering the trip's own places, unchanged) and, in Plan mode, offers `חיפוש בגוגל` — the paid half is **armed by intent**, because this is the first surface that spends money per keystroke and filtering your own list must not be the same gesture as buying an Autocomplete session; once armed it is the picker's exact behaviour (the `usePlaceSearch` core untouched, no second search path). A result card says only what the relay returns (name + secondary address, a neutral badge, a free `place_id` deep-link to vet it) — `plan-mode-v1.html`'s `4.5★ · 1.2 ק״מ · category glyph` were all fiction against the shipped pipe (ADR-0111 defers the ★, a prediction has no coords, types aren't in the mask). `＋ אולי` **is** the pin: one `pick` (dedup-before-spend) then an **uncategorised** idea referencing the place, so it reads as in-trip (ADR-0112) and shows on the list + shelf; the existing toast/undo covers it (undo keeps the cached row, so re-adding is free). A result already referenced is **stated, not re-addable** (`על המדף` / `כבר בטיול`). Offline the Google half is **absent, not disabled** (the near-me rule); a 429 stays a soft banner. Trip mode keeps its pure filter (on-the-ground discovery is a different query and SKU, deliberately out). One shared-state change: `verbs.addMaybe` takes an options bag and now carries `placeId` (the field `applyPark` already sent).
- **The day-aware shelf** — **done (2026-07-25, session 112; designed + built in [ADR-0116](decisions/0116-day-aware-shelf-and-idea-target-day.md) + `mockups/shelf-day-aware-v1.html`):** the shelf shipped as a flat trip-wide pool and never grew past it, which ADR-0115 made worse by putting `＋ אולי` one tap from a Google result. An idea now carries an **optional `targetDate`** (nullable, additive migration) — pencilled in, never a schedule: no time, no slot, off the timeline, out of `remaining`, freely clearable back to "someday". The shelf groups into `לְיום הזה` (that day's ideas + that day's skipped soft events) and `רעיונות` (dateless first, then ideas aimed elsewhere, each naming its day via `relativeDayLabel`, ADR-0085); a header renders only when its group has content, so a trip that never uses the field looks unchanged. One shared `lib/shelf.ts` derivation now feeds **both** hosts, which is how **ADR-0027's union finally renders in Plan mode** (the day's skipped soft events were invisible on the very surface you rebuild from). **skip and park stay two verbs**, both now reachable in both modes (`park` added to `EventCard`'s ⋯ menu, soft-only, gated where delete is), and park stopped dropping the event's **category** and now carries its **date** as the idea's target day. **Slotting:** the Trip-mode `ScheduleSheet` swaps its bare `TimePicker` for `WhenField variant="day"` (ADR-0083 — the last bespoke time-only control), so an idea can be put on Thursday without navigating there, with `minDate`/`maxDate` honouring ADR-0029's day-scope; and a shelf card can be **dragged onto a gap chip** in Plan mode (free time, no displacement — reusing the builder's existing pointer-capture drag, with the slot travelling on the element's `data-gap-*`), which makes the long-standing `גרור ליום` hint true. _Deferred:_ sorting the pool (derived fit/proximity/recency), a someday-beyond-this-trip split, dropping onto an occupied row (a ripple decision, ADR-0041), and a shelf-level filter row.
- **The Map says what happened at a place** — **done (2026-07-25, session 112; [ADR-0117](decisions/0117-map-place-outcome-states.md)):** the ahead/behind split was computed **purely from the clock** (neither `place-usage.ts` nor `Map.tsx` read `event.status` at all), so a place you **skipped** sank under `כבר היינו` claiming a visit that never happened, and a place marked done early stayed "ahead of you". `DayUsage` gains `outcome` (`done` wins over `skipped` across a day's references) + `settled` (every reference settled), both stored facts rather than clock reads; `isDayUsagePast` now treats a settled day as behind you **whatever the clock says** (a human outranks the clock). Rows carry `היינו`/`דילגנו` in the existing day-view words and the `--ok`/`--miss` status hues (ADR-0028 — amber stays on time, teal on location); a skipped-only row goes **quiet**, reusing the ambient treatment. The block header is now the neutral `מה שמאחורינו` (it holds three outcomes, only one a visit) and `מה שלפנינו` appears only when there IS a behind block. No schema change; the `pin.commitment` weights and ADR-0109 §4's colour tiebreak are deliberately untouched. _Deferred:_ an outcome **filter** facet ("what's left", "where we've been") — flagged by the ADR-0121 review as a **Phase-6 ride-along candidate**: it is a chip over data already derived, and it is worth more on a map than on a list, where seeing the remaining cluster is the point.
- **One settle control, three call sites** — **done (2026-07-31, session 189; [ADR-0139](decisions/0139-settling-an-event-from-the-map.md)'s Consequences amended in place):** `ui/domain/SettleControl` with three densities (`prompt` / `sheet` / `compact`), each host keeping its own placement rule, which is what "align the vocabulary, not the geometry" turned out to mean in code. **The drift was worse than the two symptoms the line named** — four, not two: the day view's skip had no mark _and_ no hue beside a ✓ that had both, the pair was worded `היינו` / **`דלג`** (a record beside an instruction, so the two answers to `היינו שם?` were not the same kind of thing — both are records now, which also makes `he.ts`'s own claim that the Map "reuses the day view's words" true for the first time), and the focus ring was teal on the card and violet in the sheet, two bespoke rules that were each half of the app's existing `.app[data-mode='plan']` idiom. `compact` keeps one thing to itself and it is a density call: its pair holds the hues back until touched, because on that row the amber wash is already doing the asking. **Parity was measured rather than assumed** (old markup under the old stylesheets vs new under the new): `compact` and the settled tag identical, `sheet` +1px and `prompt` +2px — both because the skip button finally has an icon to be as tall as — and the widened `prompt` strip does not overflow down to a 280px card. Two `!important`s went with the move. _Left standing, deliberately:_ the **outcome statement** is still drawn three ways (`PlanDay`'s `.bld-settle` marker + its `softTag`, `EventCard`'s `.wp-event-tag-done`, and the control's own tag) — one fact in three geometries, and folding a `⋯`-slot marker into a control whose other densities are button pairs would be aligning the geometry, which is what this work was told not to do. A **fourth** copy is the trigger.
- **The mockups' inlined app CSS has drifted from the shipped stylesheets** (measured 2026-07-31, session 189, while re-running the inliner for the settle extraction) — [ADR-0097](decisions/0097-mockup-catalog-out-of-root-claude-md.md)'s contract is that a mockup renders the app's **real** css and that `mockups/tools/inline-app-css.mjs` is re-run after any manifest stylesheet changes, which `design/mockups.md` repeats in every catalog entry. Against **unchanged** sources, re-running it across the catalog rewrites **~16.6k lines over 14 files** — so the rule has not been held for some time and those files are showing stale css, `map-google-pins-v1.html` worst by an order of magnitude. Not swept into session 189: a ~200-line refactor cannot carry 16k lines of someone else's drift, and the two are impossible to review together. Only `map-settle-from-canvas-v2.html` was regenerated there, because it was the one file already clean, so its diff is provably that session's own. Take this as one branch that regenerates the catalog and nothing else — and if the rule keeps slipping, the answer is a CI check or the pre-commit hook the tool's header already says it is safe for, not another reminder in the docs. (The `design-mockups` skill now states the re-run step at the point of authoring — [ADR-0175](decisions/0175-the-mockup-procedure-is-a-skill.md) — which stops new files drifting, and does nothing about the 14 already stale.)
- **The Map's `אולי` facet is the shelf** — **done (2026-07-26, session 126; [ADR-0119](decisions/0119-map-maybes-facet-is-the-shelf.md)):** three reported bugs on one chip. An idea pencilled in for a day had **no day facet** (`buildPlaceUsageIndex` passed `days: []` for every `MaybeItem`), so "maybe today" was dropped by the day scope Trip mode opens on — ADR-0116 §1's promised consequence, never built; it now contributes one **clock-free** day (no `at`/`until`/`sortOrder`/`eventId`, so it ranks as untimed and never reaches the timeline), stated on the row in a **neutral** tag because amber is time & commitment (ADR-0028). The facet read `isMaybe` (an unconsumed idea), so a **skipped soft event** — the shelf's other half (ADR-0027 §2), and what an idea becomes when it is scheduled then skipped — showed under its type chip and never under `אולי`: `PlaceUsage` gains `isParked` and the facet reads `isOnShelf = isMaybe || isParked` (soft only, the rule `lib/shelf.ts` already groups by), while `isMaybe` keeps its narrow meaning for research's `על המדף` badge. And each facet's count now reflects the other (the `אולי` count follows the picked type; the type chips + `הכל` count only shelf places while the toggle is on), so a chip can no longer promise rows the list won't render. Plus the same-date merge tiebreak stated once (`primaryRef`): a clockless reference never steals an untimed event's row wording. One derivation (ADR-0110 §2), no schema change.
- **An undated place is not in the past** — **done (2026-07-26, session 127; [ADR-0109](decisions/0109-map-tab-design.md) session-127 amendment):** reported straight after session 126 — "maybes that aren't in any day still show up as 'in the past' on the place list". Nothing on the row said past; the **header above it** did: session 110 put a dayless reference **last, in neither block**, and the list marks only where a block **starts**, so everything below `מה שמאחורינו` inherited it. The blocks are now a named vocabulary both the ordering and the headers read (`PLACE_BLOCK`/`placeBlock`), so a header can't claim a row the comparator put elsewhere; there are **three** — `מה שלפנינו` → `ללא יום` → `מה שמאחורינו` — with the undated group **between** the two (nothing about it has passed, and it makes no claim on the near future either), which keeps session 106's "never above today's schedule" fix intact. A one-block list renders **no** header, generalizing ADR-0117 §3's ahead-header rule rather than adding a second special case. Near-me untouched.
- **Embedded map — the visual pass + two open questions** (Phase 6 shipped; the epic is closed) — **built 2026-07-26, session 133** to [ADR-0121](decisions/0121-embedded-map-phase-6-design.md) (read the ADR for the design; the session note carries what the build learned). What is left is **not code**: (a) the **human visual pass** on a machine that has the browser key — it has now found three defects the suite could not (the build vars never reaching the Docker build, the opening camera swallowed by §7's containment guard, and the camera framing the ghost tier), so treat it as a real step rather than a formality; what is still unseen is how the canvas LOOKS — the fitted zoom on a real day, the 64px top inset, the connector, the sheet's motion; (b) the **map-style visual pass**: the day style is imported and has been through three rounds on a real phone ([ADR-0125](decisions/0125-map-canvas-terrain-vocabulary.md) — warm land against cool water, a land-cover terrain vocabulary, Google's sights back on as achromatic pins), but its **terrain and sights layers have not been seen rendered** — judge `forest` strength, how loud the grey sight pins read at city zoom, whether `recreation.peak` earns its pin, and what the lines radiating from Genoa's port actually are; the **night** style is still unimported and inert until dark mode ships; (c) the three `VITE_` build vars on Railway **staging**, where the tab is list-only until then — and note the coupling that cost production a deploy: a `VITE_` var set on the service reaches the container but **not** the bundle unless the `Dockerfile` declares a matching `ARG`, since Vite inlines these at build time (`architecture/deployment.md`); and (d) the two questions ADR-0121 left **open by decision**, to judge on a real rendered map rather than on paper — whether proximity promotes an out-of-scope **ghost** pin to a full one, and whether a **pin tap** opens an info window (today it selects, and the sheet's row is the answer). Clustering stays out with a stated revisit trigger: a real trip where all-days scope is routinely unreadable at the fitted zoom (§6).
- **Free Google map layers — transit (and traffic)** (**dropped for now**, owner call 2026-07-26) — `TransitLayer` draws the transit **network**, not directions: it cannot show A→B at all. Reasoning and the terms for reopening are in [ADR-0121](decisions/0121-embedded-map-phase-6-design.md) §14.
- **The Map's filters, stated once, and `מה נשאר` fixed** — **done (2026-07-27, session 144; [ADR-0124](decisions/0124-map-filters-scope-facets-and-what-is-left.md)):** reported twice in one session — "I'm not sure what stays at `מה נשאר` and what not… I'm not sure how compound filtering works", then "I see past events in `מה נשאר` and I really don't understand what's the usage here". The first was a documentation failure (the answer lived across ADR-0110 §4, 0109 §2, 0119, 0117 and 0121 §9, each right about its piece and none saying what the tab does); the second a design defect. ADR-0121 §9 defined the toggle as "hide everything `settled`", but settling is a **manual tap** 0027 §1/0018 both refuse to automate, so on a real trip almost nothing is settled and the filter hid almost nothing. Now **two things close a place — a human closing it or the clock** — which is `isDayUsagePast`, already both and already reading `settled` first (so a stop marked `היינו` for tonight goes now, not at 20:00), giving one sentence: **`מה נשאר` hides exactly what the list files under `מה שמאחורינו`**, `isPlaceLeft` reading `placeBlock` rather than a second copy of it. Gate moves to `hasBehind`. **A second defect fell out of writing the model down:** all-days read every place off `days[0]`, so the hotel you sleep in **tonight** sat under `מה שמאחורינו` with a desaturated pin from its second night on (session 137 took that fade off the _ambient_ tier; all-days never reached that tier, so `PIN_TIER.behind` re-applied it through `skipped`) — `placeDay` now takes a context and resolves the day a place is **live** on, while `buildPinOrderIndex` still passes **no clock** so a tick cannot renumber a pin. Plus: a surfaced ghost's way in (`forceDay` reached the row's day but not `refEntriesFor`), an all-days stay naming its next edge, self-clearing toggles, and a three-way empty state (empty trip / empty **day**, offers all-days / over-narrow filter, names the facets and offers to clear them).
- **A place has no way to become an event or a booking — the map half** — **done (2026-07-31, session 185; [ADR-0135](decisions/0135-a-place-becomes-an-event-or-a-booking.md), designed session 181, narrowed 183):** the way-in block's reference list gained a footer with one `.map-addmaybe` control (`＋ שיבוץ ליום`, 44×44 scoped to `.map-refs-foot`), `scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' })` on a row selection — which §8 had measured as owed **before** this phase, since the block already overflowed the `half` sheet at 360 with two references — and the Map became a host of `EventForm`, with the `usePlaceErrandReturn<EventFormDraft>('event', 'map', …)` session 165's rule demands. §3's finding held under the build: this is **not** the errand run backwards, so none of ADR-0134's draft machinery was needed. `soleIdeaFor` (`lib/place-refs.ts`) carries §5 — exactly one idea is consumed through `verbs.schedule`, two or more consume nothing, and it is deliberately **not** day-scoped; that case is the reproduction and it fails when relaxed to "any idea". The footer is absent under a place errand (§7). Phase 11 and ADR-0131 §9's long press were **not** taken.
- **An event has no way to become a booking** — **done (2026-07-31, session 185; [ADR-0136](decisions/0136-an-event-can-also-be-booked.md), extracted session 183, trigger replaced 184):** `EventForm` gained one `יש הזמנה` row on every host. Off saves an event; on **also** creates the `Booking` and links it — three write shapes behind one toast and one undo (`verbs.book`): a create sends the `event` seed and the server makes the pair, a conversion sends none and then patches `bookingId`, and a shelf schedule adds the idea's consume. The undo **deletes the booking before restoring the place**, or ADR-0048's invariant nulls it straight back out. The kind is derived at create and **preserved** on conversion (§4) — `EventForm` gained the `kindTouched` it never had. **Two ADR amendments made in place while building:** §1's "no new primitive" was wrong, so `ui/primitives/ToggleChip` was extracted and all four Map chips migrated onto it (costed and put to the owner first, per rule 8's ask-first clause); and §2 now **asks** the booking type for `transport` alone, because `EventCategory` has one `transport` while `BookingType` has flight, train and other — the "one tap on the category" §2 promised did not exist.
- **An undone schedule leaves the idea consumed server-side** — **done (2026-07-31, session 186):** `POST /maybe-items/:id/restore`, the inverse of `consume`, beside it and idempotent for the same reason (an outbox flush can replay an undo that already landed). A **dedicated action** rather than the `consumed` field on the PATCH this line originally proposed — reading the service changed the answer: `update` is ADR-0116 §1's day-aim, _a pencil mark, not a schedule_, and its `apply` writes `targetDate` unconditionally, so a patch carrying only `consumed` would have silently cleared the idea's day. Frontend: one `restoreConsumed` helper both undo paths call, so a plain schedule and a booked save cannot drift; the `create` undo descriptor gained the `maybeId` it consumed. Queued through the outbox with a cache mirror, so an undo made offline still lands and the shelf shows it at once. Three frontend reproductions + three backend specs, all verified to fail against the shipped bug. **Also fixed on the way:** `t.sync.verb` was `as Record<string, string>`, so a verb missing from it rendered a queued failure with no name — now `satisfies Record<OutboxVerb, string>`.
- **The `*Touched` guards want generalising** — **done (2026-07-31, session 186; [ADR-0136](decisions/0136-an-event-can-also-be-booked.md) Consequences amended in place):** `lib/useDerivedField` is the one mechanism — `value` / `set` / `redrive` / `reset` / `touched`. Counting them properly found **seven**, not three: `EventForm`'s three, `BookingSheet`'s two, and `CreateTrip`'s `nameTouched`/`iconTouched`, which auto-suggest from the destination and nobody had counted. Kept rather than flattened, both because they are load-bearing: an **initial value can already count as chosen** (ADR-0136 §4 — a hook tracking only "did the user click" would silently harden a soft event on conversion), and **`reset`**, which only `BookingSheet`'s ✨ revert uses, so the next field wanting one does not have to invent it. `redrive` returns the value **now in force**, which is how one handler re-derives three fields without reading unflushed state. Dirtiness deliberately stayed at the call sites, where the two forms disagree on purpose. Seven value-plus-flag pairs → seven hook calls, 13 fewer `useState` calls, and **no test changed** — the migration is behaviour-preserving, confirmed by trap-checking that removing the guard still fails ADR-0136's own soft-event reproduction. A **nullable-override slot** was considered and rejected for these: the values are rendered, so every read site would become `value ?? derive()`.
- **Whether proximity promotes a ghost pin** (open fork from the ADR-0121 review, deliberately unresolved) — a place outside the day scope but 50 m away is nearer than anything in scope and is invisible to near-me's sort. Near-me sharpens the question rather than settling it; judge it on a real rendered map.
- **Routes and travel time — DESIGNED 2026-08-24, nothing built.** [ADR-0205](decisions/0205-routes-are-computed-not-bought-and-a-route-is-a-cache.md) (the substrate: Valhalla behind a `RouteProvider` port, a `RouteLeg` cache keyed on rounded coordinates and outside the change log, a per-mode routing gate) + [ADR-0206](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md) (what it says: ten principles, nine ranked V1 features, the V2 deferrals). **Both `Accepted` 2026-08-25 — M0 closed** (read ADR-0206 §Z and ADR-0205 §Y, the owner's answers changed three things). The only open substrate question is the provider; ADR-0205 §Y1 weighs it and records a reversible default, so nothing is blocked. **Progress lives on the milestone board, not here:** [`planning/2026-08-24-routes-epic-milestone-board.md`](planning/2026-08-24-routes-epic-milestone-board.md), M0–M12. Prune this line when M12 closes.
- **`Collapsible`'s transition is a literal while the caret it moves with rides the ramp** ([ADR-0206 §AL10](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md), found building M8b) — `ui/primitives/collapsible.css` transitions at a `0.32s` literal and every caret that opens one rotates on `--t-base` (240ms), so the two halves of one gesture are visibly out of step. It has been that way since the primitive shipped and now has four hosts, which is why it is a line rather than a fix inside M8b: the repair belongs in the primitive (ADR-0140's "timings come from the ramp"), and touching it moves every disclosure in the app at once.
- **Seven more `Icon` glyphs have a facing and do not mirror in RTL** (owner, 2026-08-27; [ADR-0138 §10.4](decisions/0138-the-row-menu-is-one-surface-and-icons-are-ui.md)) — the owner's rule is _"all glyphs that have a direction should have RTL variants"_, and M8a applied it to the two mode glyphs that have one (`walking`, `cycling`) via `scaleX(var(--dir))` plus a named `MIRRORED` allowlist. The audit in [`mockups/the-mode-set-and-transit-declared-v1.html`](../mockups/the-mode-set-and-transit-declared-v1.html) §5c measured **all 58** entries and found seven more: `exit`, `undo`, `external`, `navigate`, `search`, `bracket`, `ticket`. Two are the same class as `NavArrow` (leaving and going back are directions of travel) and two are a real argument rather than an oversight — a magnifier's handle, a compass needle — so each wants its own look. **Do not reach for "mirror everything asymmetric":** 30 of 58 are asymmetric and only 9 have a facing, and `clock` mirrored reads a different time in an app whose subject is time (§10.2). Deliberately not folded into M8a: seven glyphs across eight screens is the quiet widening rule 8 forbids.
- **An infeasible travel leg should keep its mode glyph** (owner, 2026-08-26) — **done (2026-08-27): drawn in M8a of the routes epic ([ADR-0206 §AL](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md), [`mockups/the-mode-set-and-transit-declared-v1.html`](../mockups/the-mode-set-and-transit-declared-v1.html)) and coded in M8b ([§AM](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md)).** M6a ships `icon={overrunning ? 'warn' : travelMode}`, which swaps the mode mark for a generic warning; the block already takes `tone: 'miss'`, so the swap repeats the state and costs the row its class. **Compose, do not mint the matrix** — and the drawing made that cheaper than [ADR-0206 §AK2](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md) claimed: the mark is `warn`, which already exists, so the whole set costs **one** new asset (`transit`, minted because `ticket` already means "a booking" in four screens). §AK2's stated precedent — the avatar badge — **does not exist in `ui/primitives/avatar.css`**; the idiom is `PlaceBadge`'s corner mark (ADR-0167 §1), whose 32–40px badge is `.day-trv-ic`'s box exactly. §AK3's four questions are answered in §AL4/§AL5. Tracked as **M6e**, drawn inside **M8a**, on the routes board.
- **The dev seed has no coordinates** — **done (2026-08-25; M1b of the routes epic, [board](planning/2026-08-24-routes-epic-milestone-board.md) · [ADR-0205](decisions/0205-routes-are-computed-not-bought-and-a-route-is-a-cache.md) §Z0 amended in place):** all eight `Place` rows in `backend/prisma/seed.mjs` were name-only Place-lite ([ADR-0147](decisions/0147-a-place-is-made-on-the-canvas.md)) with `lat`/`lng` `null`, because the seed predated the Places picker that fills them — harmless until the routes epic, where M4, M5, M6a/b, M7, M9 and M11 all need a routable trip and the seed could not supply one. Now every place carries its real coordinate and the zone geo-tz resolves from it, and each gate path is a named fixture with a comment table saying which: a walkable Tokyo day, the two 0.00 km pairs `ROUTE_MIN_CROW_M` was measured against, a leg refused over the driving ceiling (a feature — ADR-0206 §D4's crow-flies chip), a cross-cluster pair, and a gap travel visibly eats. **Plus a second trip**, which the line as written did not ask for and M8 needs: a bookings-derived default mode cannot be exercised by a trip of flights, a hotel and a restaurant, so the Iceland ring road carries [ADR-0162](decisions/0162-a-car-hire-is-transport-you-drive-yourself.md)'s car hire and Tokyo becomes the negative case. The measured leg table is on M1b's card.
- **`בדרך` is a device mark, not a group one** — decided and deferred (2026-08-26; M6b of the routes epic, [ADR-0206](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md) §AE2). `t.actions.onWay` finally writes state, into `frontend/src/lib/on-way.ts`: `localStorage`, keyed by trip and event, pruned after a day, and it is what withdraws §V1.4's late mark for the person who pressed it. **What it does not do is tell anybody else**, which is what the old toast claimed (`שותף לקבוצה` over a verb that wrote nothing — the copy is corrected). The group-visible version is a nullable instant on `Event`, a Prisma migration, a DTO field and a `CACHE_CHANNELS` mirror, plus a decision about whether it settles anything or merely annotates — a milestone with an ADR, not a line to smuggle into a routes card. Until it exists, what the group sees still comes only from a verb a person pressed, which is [ADR-0006](decisions/0006-no-live-location-v1.md)'s own rule.
- **`WALK_DEFAULT_MAX_SECONDS` wants the same device pass as the buffer** (2026-08-28, [ADR-0206 §AU2/§AV1/§AV5](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md)). The walk a leg may be before the app stops defaulting to it is **⁦10 minutes⁩** — the owner's own number, and their own _"probably"_ is why it is a named constant rather than a decision: _"walking should be defaulted to only when it makes sense, definitely not a four hour walk, max 10 minutes probably."_ `WALK_DEFAULT_MAX_M` (⁦700 m⁩) is the crow floor under it, for a leg whose walking time is not known yet, calibrated to the same ten minutes and rounded down. Neither touches `TRAVEL_GATE.walking.maxMeters` (⁦15 km⁩), which §Z8 raised deliberately and which limits what may be PICKED rather than what is guessed. **The judgement is "would you walk this", which is a feel call on a real day** — the same standing as `TRAVEL_BUFFER_SECONDS` and `ARRIVAL_RADIUS_MAX_M` in the line below, and it should ride the same pass. Worth measuring on a trip where the group walks a lot, which is the case a too-low number gets wrong.
- **An urban trip's default is transit, and that is blocked on transit routing** (owner, 2026-08-28, [ADR-0206 §AV5](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md)): _"or an urban trip but that's probably a whole epic so don't worry about it."_ Correct on both counts. In a dense city the honest default for a leg past a ten-minute walk is neither walking nor driving — and until §V2's transit routing exists there is nothing truthful to default TO, since a declared תחב״צ leg deliberately has no duration (§AA4). Do not approximate it by widening the walking default: that is the exact defect §AV1 closed. Take it with the transit epic.
- **The warming row's copy has not been seen on a real cold day** (2026-08-28, [ADR-0206 §AU3](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md)). `מחשב מסלול…` is drawn and measured in both themes at 360 and 390 ([`mockups/a-route-is-on-its-way-v1.html`](../mockups/a-route-is-on-its-way-v1.html) ships two alternatives as controls), but the question it cannot answer on a desktop is whether four of them at once — a cold day with four unmeasured holes — reads as reassurance or as a page of noise. If it is noise, the lever is the copy, not the placeholder: the sized slot is what stops the day shifting and has to stay.
- **A proportional half for `TRAVEL_BUFFER_SECONDS`** — the owner's "tolerance relative to the total distance", moved to where it belongs ([ADR-0207](decisions/0207-a-fix-may-withdraw-a-claim-it-may-not-make-one.md)'s Alternatives). It is **not** a fix for the late-mark report — without a position there is nothing to be tolerant of, so a flat grace only delays a wrong claim and delays the right one too — but the instinct is correct about **estimate error**: a 44-minute walk carries more of it than a 5-minute one, and the buffer is a flat 5 minutes that `travel-time.ts` itself flags as a placeholder owed to the device pass. Flat + proportional, measured on a real day. **And it now has a second reader** (2026-08-26, [ADR-0206](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md) §AH2): `TRAVEL_FIT_TOLERANCE_SECONDS` is _derived_ from this constant, because the buffer is the error bar the app has already admitted to and a shortfall inside it is indistinguishable from zero — so retuning the buffer retunes what the day calls an impossible leg. One number, two consequences, which is an argument for measuring it and not for splitting it. `ARRIVAL_RADIUS_MAX_M` (⁦2km⁩) is a judgement of the same standing and wants the same pass.
- **A day seen for the FIRST time still gains its journeys a beat after it paints** ([ADR-0206 §AT1](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md)) — **the row-reservation half is done (2026-08-28, [§AU1](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md), drawn in [`mockups/a-route-is-on-its-way-v1.html`](../mockups/a-route-is-on-its-way-v1.html)).** This line asked for exactly what §AU1 built — _"a leg we are asking about draws its block from the first paint and fills in … which needs a state the journey block does not have today (§D4 has no 'asked, not yet answered') and a decision about what such a block says while it is empty"_. It has that state now: a `WARMING` arm that renders the mode, the mode control and `מחשב מסלול…`, with a sized placeholder where the duration lands, measured at ⁦40px⁩ computing and ⁦40px⁩ answered so the row does not move. It arrived as a field report rather than as this line's own turn, and it came with the fault underneath it: the client asked once, retried once and gave up, against a cold day's three matrix calls paced ⁦1/s⁩ — so on top of the silence there was often no answer coming at all until the surface remounted. **What is left of this line is the FIRST paint, unchanged:** the hold is on this device's cache and deliberately not on the network (root `CLAUDE.md` rule 5), so a day nobody has ever measured still paints, then fills its blocks in when the matrix lands. That is now a block gaining a NUMBER rather than a day gaining ⁦162px⁩ of blocks, which is the smaller half of the original defect — and M10's precomputed pack still makes most days warm before they are opened.
- **A leave-by should be floored to the nearest 5 minutes** — decided, not built (2026-08-26; owner's question, [ADR-0206](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md) §AH6). `יציאה 14:11` is false precision: the duration it derives from is hedged (`~44 דק׳`) and the instant printed from it is not, so §D5's hedge evaporates exactly where it matters. **Floored, never nearest** — rounding to nearest can round _later_ (14:13 → 14:15), telling you to leave after the honest instant and eating `TRAVEL_BUFFER_SECONDS`; flooring only ever gives time back. It belongs in `lib/hero-travel.ts`'s `heroLeaveBy`, because the board's countdown tile, the lifted hero and the day's journey block all read that one function so they cannot name three minutes for one departure — which is also why it is not a formatter change: it moves **when the late mark fires** (§V1.4), up to four minutes earlier. Wants that consequence looked at on a real day.
- **A stop that has already started cannot be postponed, and ripple is why** — decided and deferred (2026-08-26; M6d of the routes epic, [ADR-0208](decisions/0208-a-claim-needs-something-to-stand-on.md)'s Alternatives). `דחה` shifts an event by a fixed step from its **own start**, so on a stop the clock has passed the target lands behind the clock too and the server refuses it (`MOVE_INTO_PAST`) — which is the commonest moment anybody presses it. Re-anchoring the nudge to **now** would make it work, and is blocked on ripple: the suggestion shifts each following soft event by the **mover's own delta**, which is exactly what preserves the gaps between them, so a delta measured from the clock rather than from a stale start can be hours and one tap on `הזזה` would push the rest of the day into tomorrow. Ripple has to learn a **clearing** delta first — and that is not obviously an improvement in the ordinary case, where the mover's delta keeps a 30-minute gap and a clearing delta eats it. Wants a real day and a decision, not a patch. Until then the refusal is at least legible: the toast says why, before anything is queued.
- **Plan mode's slot PICKER still states the raw gap** — **done (2026-08-27; M9 of the routes epic, [ADR-0206 §AN4](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md), drawn in [`mockups/a-travel-time-between-two-points-v2.html`](../mockups/a-travel-time-between-two-points-v2.html) §5c).** It is a lookup rather than a second derivation: a `DayPosition` names the rows either side of it, which is exactly the pair `useDayTravelReads` is keyed on, so the sheet now reads the number the chip already shows for the same hole. `earnsChipAt` asks the **corrected** number (§AG5), so a hole a walk eats loses its offer entirely rather than advertising nought. **Two positions keep the raw hole and both are §D4, not a compromise:** the day's two edges have a row on one side only, and a position joined around the row being MOVED (`dayPositions`' `exclude`) has two rows that are not adjacent on the day as it stands — neither has a leg to ask about, and the app does not invent a walk it did not measure.
- **The journey block's arrival moves the day's rows, and it cannot be reserved** — measured, not fixed (2026-08-26; M6a of the routes epic, [ADR-0206](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md) §AF7). A hole with no travel estimate is ⁦20px⁩ of free-time strip; with one it is a ⁦58px⁩ block, and the estimate arrives from a request — so on a cold day-open every hole below the first grows by ⁦38px⁩ once the matrix lands. **Reserving the space is the one option that is worse:** §D4 requires absence to cost nothing, and an empty ⁦58px⁩ hole in every gap of every day is exactly what that rule refuses. Cached after the first visit, so a revisit does not shift. **M10 shipped the fix for a downloaded trip (2026-08-27, [§AO](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md)):** the offline route pack hydrates Dexie on the Map's mount, so the day's estimates are present before the first paint and the holes never grow. What is left of this line is the trip nobody downloaded, where the first open still shifts — and reserving the space is still the one option that is worse.
- **`--faint` on free time, and teal on a chip label, both miss AA — the app-wide contrast sweep** (measured 2026-08-26 by M6a, [ADR-0206](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md) §AF6; the same sweep the ~40 stale `:hover` rules are on, `frontend/CLAUDE.md`). The shipped `.day-gap-lbl` is **⁦3.03:1⁩** in light theme and the journey block's own free-time run is ⁦3.42:1⁩ — so the block improves it and both miss the ⁦4.5⁩ a sentence owes; `--muted` on that ground measures ⁦5.23:1⁩ and is the obvious replacement. Same for `.wp-event-act.go`'s teal label at ⁦3.35:1⁩, which the block's `בדרך` chip matches at ⁦3.07:1⁩ deliberately. **Neither was changed inside M6a on purpose:** the block would then have disagreed with the row beside it, and free-time ink is a decision about the whole day surface rather than about one slot. Wants one pass over both, with the numbers above as its starting point.
- **The board's countdown tile widens on `H:MM` and breaks a long `הבא בתור` title** — measured, not fixed (2026-08-26; [ADR-0206](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md) §AE5). At 360px the tile is `74px` under every unit word it can carry, and `76.58px` the moment its VALUE steps to `H:MM` — which is arm 1's shipped behaviour for any next event an hour or more out, not something the leave-by swap introduced. A long title then wraps to two lines (`21px` → `41px`). The fix is a `.wp-board-next-row` question (the title's own `min-width`, or a fixed tile width across all rungs) and wants measuring against the widest real title rather than a guess.
- **Transit routing** — V2 of the routes epic ([ADR-0206](decisions/0206-a-travel-time-belongs-between-two-points.md) §V2), and its own epic rather than a milestone. Measured: Senso-ji → Tokyo Station is **73 min walking and 25 min by train**, so a walking-only ETA is not imprecise, it is wrong in the way that makes someone miss a booking. Nothing free is usable — the FOSSGIS Valhalla server has no GTFS tiles (`multimodal` errors) and Transitous is barred by its own terms (non-commercial, open-source applications only). The un-blocked path is self-hosting MOTIS (MIT: OSM + GTFS, street and transit routing), a second service and a per-region feed pipeline. **M0 answered "I can live without transit on V1" (2026-08-25)**, so this stays V2 and the board stands as planned. Note it is also the one thing that forces self-hosting the router — GTFS tiles cannot come from anyone else's build (ADR-0205 §Y1). Write the ADR when it is taken.
- **Multi-zone time model** (ADR-0107, dependent workstream — **building in slices**) — fixes the single `Trip.timezone` that mis-renders zone-crossing transport. Places carry a timezone (offline lat/lng→zone, cached on the row) resolved in Phase 1; then per-event **sticky** display zones by priority (place > itinerary segment > demoted trip primary/destination zone), transport rendering start/end in origin/destination zones, an editable zone chip, and `Trip.timezone` demoted to the trip primary zone (the origin/home zone is a segment concept from the outbound flight, not this field). Display/authoring/segment layer runs alongside Phases 2–3, independent of the map render. No instant migration (instants are absolute). **FE layout (ADR-0110):** the resolver lives in `lib/places.ts` (reading cached `Place.timezone` only, never computing); thread the resolved zone where `trip.timezone` is passed to `zonedIso`/`formatTime` in `lib/time.ts`; the zone chip is an addition to `WhenField`/`TimeField` (one chip for a placed event, two for transport) opening the **shared `ZonePicker`** (built in ADR-0113). **`Event.displayTimezone` store-vs-derive resolved (ADR-0110 §94-99): a manual override, derived by default.**
  - **Slice 1 — data model + resolver — done (2026-07-24, session 88):** nullable `Event.displayTimezone` (the override slot) across `@waypoint/shared` (`tripEventSchema` + event field schema), `schema.prisma` + migration, `TripsService`/`toEventDto` + `EventsService` create/update passthrough; and the pure, clock-free resolver in `lib/places.ts` — `tripZoneCrossings` (transport whose endpoint zones differ → a departure-instant crossing) + `segmentZoneAt` (origin before the crossing, destination at/after) + `eventDisplayZones(event, {bookings, places, crossings, primaryZone}) → {start, end}` (override > place [transport: start=`fromPlace`, end=`toPlace`] > segment > trip primary). Fully unit-tested; **no display wiring yet** (the schema keeps `displayTimezone` plain-optional here — slice 4's chip widens it to nullable to clear the override).
  - _(Session 100 corrected slices 2/3: the day's ambient zone and the live "now" were keyed to the transport crossings alone, so every day after an outbound flight was framed in that flight's destination zone — see the ADR-0107 session-100 amendment.)_
  - **Slice 2 — display wiring — done (sessions 89–90):** both day timelines (`DayView` **and** Plan-mode `PlanDay`) render each end in its resolved zone and show a **time-shift delta pill** (`🕐 +6 ש׳`) — destination-vs-origin for a crossing, else vs the day's ambient zone (segment-at-noon, else trip primary) — only when the shift is non-zero (single-zone trips stay bare). Session 89 first shipped a `· city` label; **session 90 replaced it with the delta pill** after a design review (English IANA city in an RTL row + cramped wrap; and a traveler wants _how far the clock jumps_, not the zone name) — see the ADR-0107 session-90 amendment. `eventZones`/`eventEdgeZone` (`lib/places.ts`) + `zoneOffsetMinutes`/`formatZoneDelta`/`crossesMidnightZoned` (`lib/time.ts`); `EventCard`/`TransitionRow`/`BuilderRow` gained optional zone props (absent → base `tz`, no pill). **Not yet wired: the board hero (`GlanceCard` now/next)** — its zone is the live-"now" context, so it lands with slice 3.
  - **Slice 3 — live "now" segment tracking — done (2026-07-24, session 96):** `currentZone(nowMs, crossings, primaryZone)` resolves the zone of the segment you're in; the trip's crossings are derived once in `trip-state` (`zoneCrossings`) so no surface recomputes them and the clock tick never re-derives them. Trip mode frames "today", the day-strip anchor, the day scope, the now-line clock and the board through it — the calendar day rolls at the **current segment's** midnight (ADR-0107 §4); Plan mode stays on the trip primary. **ADR-0029 refinement:** a day is read-only once **its own** ambient zone says it is over, not when the live zone rolls — otherwise crossing east mid-flight would lock the day you're still flying through. _(The board hero's per-event zones followed in session 97.)_
  - **Slice 3b — the hero + glance read per-event zones — done (2026-07-24, session 97):** the last single-`tz` surfaces. Every board slot (now / also-now / group-split rows / next / the in-transit flight) renders its times in that **event's own** zones and carries the shift pill; the in-transit ends read origin-then-destination (so a `07:15 → 11:00` flight can't misread as 3h45) and shorten their place names like every other glanceable surface (session 95). The glance rail's anchors carry per-anchor zones too — `buildDayGlance` takes an optional `ZoneContext` and each anchor gets its own zone(s) + delta, with the "+1" decided **per-zone** (`crossesMidnightZoned`), and its shift measured against the **day's** ambient (a day surface), not the live zone. The pill itself became one component (`ui/ZoneShiftPill`) replacing the three copy-pasted surface classes, and `dayAmbientZone` replaced the segment-at-noon computation duplicated in `DayView`/`Home` (`DAY_NOON`).
  - **Slice 4a — per-endpoint form authoring — done (2026-07-24, session 91):** `BookingSheet` now enters + reads each time field **in its own endpoint's zone** (ADR-0107 §2) — a flight's departure in its origin, its arrival in its destination; a single-place booking in its place — so the form and the day view finally agree (the arrival showed 14:00 trip-zone while the view showed 11:00 destination-zone). `WhenField` span gained an optional `endTimeZone` (correct cross-zone duration read-out); `buildSpanSeed` gained an `endTimeZone` arg; `BookingSheet` resolves `startZone`/`endZone` from the picked places (`placeTimezone`, now exported) and threads them through read (`isoToTimeInput`/`isoToDateTimeLocal`), the end-before-start check, and save. A read-only **`ZoneNote`** under the schedule names each zone + the shift, so which zone a typed time means is never ambiguous. Changing a pick keeps the typed wall-clock and re-interprets on save (§8). No override writer yet.
  - **Slice 4b — editable zone chip + `displayTimezone` override writer — done (2026-07-24, session 98):** completes ADR-0107. `EventForm` now authors in the event's **resolved** zone (override > place > segment > trip primary) instead of `trip.timezone`, reads an existing event back in that same zone, and states it in a new `ui/primitives/ZoneChip` (`🕐 Tokyo · GMT+9 ▾` → shared `ZonePicker`) mounted through `WhenField`'s day variant. A pick writes the **manual override**; a `חזרה לאזור אוטומטי` reset sends `null`, handing the event back to the derivation. The chip is read-only once a place is picked (the place is the honest edit, §3). `displayTimezone` widened to `timezoneSchema.nullish()` (a bad zone is now a 400, not a `RangeError`); the null-to-`undefined` coercion generalized from trips' `coerceTripPatch` into `coerceClearedFields` and moved **inside** `applyToRow`, so no entity's cleared field can cache a `null` again.
  - **Slice 4c — the chip in booking forms, per endpoint — done (2026-07-24, session 99):** editing anything with a booking routes to `BookingSheet`, which had no zone control, so a **coordless Place-lite** (offline / no Google match → no `timezone`) silently fell back to the trip primary with no way to correct it. `Event.displayTimezone` couldn't serve: it collapses both ends onto one zone, which a crossing can't use.
